## Document 004: 10月企業物価指数 ～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月企業物価指数 ～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 11 月 12 日

1/2 
10 月企業物価指数 
～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  阿原 健一郎 
（℡：050-5470-4868）

1. 国内企業物価指数は幾分加速 
 本日公表された 10 月の国内企業物価指数は前年比＋7.1％と、前月（同＋6.8％）から 
伸びが幾分加速した。前年比寄与度で内訳を見ると、玄米や豚肉などの「農林水産物」のマイナ
ス寄与が前月から拡大した一方、「電力・都市ガス・水道」や「石油・石炭製品」、「化学製品」の
プラス寄与が前月から拡大し、ヘッドラインを押し上げた（図表１）。 
 背景には、原油やナフサの価格上昇が石油製品や化学製品へ波及していることに加え、電力に
おいても、過去の輸入燃料価格の上昇がタイムラグを伴って燃料費調整単価に反映され、全体を
押し上げていることがある。農林水産物の価格下落が一部で下押し要因となっているものの、エ
ネルギー関連を中心としたコスト上昇圧力は依然として根強い。

２. 輸入物価指数はわずかに加速 
 10 月の輸入物価指数は前年比＋24.8％と、前月（同＋23.8％）から伸びがわずかに加速した。
「石油・石炭・天然ガス」のプラス寄与が前月から拡大し、ヘッドラインを押し上げた（図表
２）。 
 為替変動の影響を除いた契約通貨ベースでも同＋19.2％と、前月（同＋17.3％）から伸びが拡大
しており、海外の資源価格上昇が輸入物価を押し上げている。輸入コストの増加が時間差を伴っ
て国内の製品価格に波及することを踏まえると、先行きも価格転嫁を通じて消費者物価への上昇
圧力が残る可能性がある。

以 上

りません。

## Document 047: 20261123_投資週報_AI基本面維持韌性，融資、利率與監管風險升溫

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261123_投資週報_AI基本面維持韌性，融資、利率與監管風險升溫.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 23 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國擴大 AI 晶片管制範圍，全球算力供應鏈面臨重組：美國 BIS 於 11/16 公布新規，將先進
晶片管制由實體出口延伸至第三國資料中心提供的遠端算力服務；向中國等受限地區提供合計
逾 1,200 萬 TPP (總處理效能) 算力的業者，出口特定晶片及設備須取得許可，並新增對東南
亞及巴西等地的出口許可要求。新規可能提高輝達、超微及海外資料中心合規成本，美系大型
雲端業者相對受惠，後續關注訂單轉移及全球 AI 算力部署變化。

◼ 輝達財報維持高速成長，SEC 會計調查疑慮重創市場信心：輝達 11/17 公布季度營收達 1,158
億美元，年增 103%，其中資料中心營收年增 110%至 1,076 億美元，顯示 AI 運算需求仍具
韌性。然而，11/20 Reuters 報導 SEC 正在檢視輝達可能涉及的會計問題，輝達當日股價重挫
14%，全週下跌約 23%。市場疑慮由 AI 投資回報延伸至財務透明度及企業治理，加劇半導體
產業賣壓，惟具體會計違規事項尚未獲確認。
◼ Fed 會議紀要釋出偏鷹訊號，市場重新評估升息風險：Fed 於 10 月 FOMC 會議中，以 10 比
2 票決議維持政策利率於 3.75%至 4.00%，兩名反對委員主張升息 1 碼；9 月整體及核心 PCE
通膨年增率估達 3.6%及 3.1%，顯示關稅、能源與 AI 投資需求仍帶來物價壓力。經濟與就業
雖具韌性，但通膨朝 2%目標回落的進展仍不穩定，市場須留意利率維持高檔甚至再升息的可
能，美債殖利率及高評價股票走勢將更受通膨與 Fed 政策影響。

市場展望
◼ 短線審慎，中期審慎偏正向，關注企業獲利與資金面變化：全球科技股修正已由評價調整擴及
企業治理、政策及資金面，市場風險偏好明顯下降，短期賣壓仍待消化。惟 AI 運算需求尚未
明顯反轉，主要 CSP 持續投入 AI 基礎建設，中長期成長動能仍具支撐。因此，美國科技股及
具 AI 供應鏈優勢的台、日市場，短期維持審慎，中期審慎偏正向。後續應聚焦企業獲利、訂
單及現金流，並關注 CSP 資本支出、Fed 政策與信用風險，配置以技術領先、財務穩健企業
為主。
◼ 市場避險情緒上升，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：Fed 政策與通膨不確定性仍可能造成利率
波動，但股市風險偏好下降推升美債避險需求，當前殖利率亦具配置吸引力。考量殖利率水準、
利率敏感度及信用品質，中天期美國公債與投資等級公司債風險報酬相對均衡，展望偏正向。
超長天期公債仍須留意財政赤字及期限溢價風險，投資級債整體信用品質穩健，惟須持續關注
AI 融資疑慮及信用利差擴大風險。

市場焦點
美國
• 10月零售銷售月增0.2%，整體增幅略緩，但與GDP高度相關的控制組零售銷售月增0.6%，顯
示高利率環境下消費需求仍具韌性，內需仍是支撐美國經濟的重要力量，然而可支配所得成
長趨緩、儲蓄率維持低檔，且家庭支出連續多月高於薪資成長，反映部分消費來自信用擴張
而非所得改善。整體而言，美國消費短期仍支撐經濟，但家庭購買力壓力正逐漸積累，未來
內需續航力仍有待觀察。
中國
• H200晶片的出口管制反覆政策變化，促使中國企業加速開發本土替代產品，加上中國經濟正
從房地產開發，轉向高科技帶動經濟成長，彭博預估2026年中國高科技產業的規模將達到27
兆元人民幣，占GDP的18.3%。總結來說，美中科技戰將使全球AI 供應鏈逐漸形成兩套體系，
以美國為核心，另一套以中國自主晶片為核心，長期而言這將改變半導體、AI 伺服器及資料中

心產業的競爭格局。
AI
資本支出
• 半導體研究機構Semi Analysis下修未來數年全球AI加速器出貨、資本支出、HBM需求及電
力需求預測，反映AI產業正由GPU主導的高速擴張階段，逐步轉向多元運算架構與成本效率
競爭，預估輝達受到的衝擊相對較大；博通、AMD，以及Google TPU、Amazon Trainium
等平台，則因客製化晶片與成本效益優勢，展現相對韌性。這並不代表ASIC將全面取代GPU，
而是AI算力市場的供應來源將更加多元化。

消費韌性延續、購買力承壓，美國內需強勁背後隱憂浮現
• 整體零售增幅放緩，核心消費仍具韌性：美國10月零售銷售月增0.2%，低於9月的0.8%，但與市場預期相符；與GDP
消費支出較相關的控制組零售銷售月增0.6%，優於預估0.4%，且9月數據上修，顯示核心消費仍具支撐力，非店面零售、
建材及醫療保健用品銷售維持成長，反映美國內需尚未明顯轉弱，仍是支撐經濟成長的重要動能。
• 實質所得成長受限，家庭消費續航力面臨考驗：零售銷售成長仍須留意物價因素，10月名目銷售增幅未必代表實質消費
同步擴張；9月實質可支配所得月減0.3%，儲蓄率降至3.8%，顯示家庭購買力與儲蓄緩衝能力承壓，儘管低收入家庭近
期支出仍具韌性，但通膨侵蝕實質所得，加上高利率環境持續，後續消費動能仍面臨考驗。

• 後市觀察：美國消費短期仍具韌性，但物價與融資成本偏高，可能逐步壓抑非必要性支出；建議股票優先布局具品牌優
勢、定價能力及獲利穩定的民生必需品與醫療保健類股，債券則以短天期美債及高信評債券為主，降低升息環境下的價
格波動風險。
近三月美國零售銷售月增率變化(%)

美國通膨黏著依舊，Fed緊縮政策提高升息預期
• 通膨來源輪動，核心PCE動能再度升溫：彭博經濟研究預估10月核心PCE月增0.29%、年增3.2%，三個月年化增幅由
2.9%升至3.5%。儘管AI相關硬體、軟體及機票價格回落，有助抵銷部分通膨壓力，但處方藥、餐飲服務、地面運輸及部
分商品價格接續上漲，使核心通膨維持高檔，顯示通膨壓力持續輪動，尚未有效消退。
• 通膨黏著性升高，市場轉向升息定價：過去一年漲幅逾3%的PCE分項占比預估由49%升至51%，反映通膨改善仍不穩固；
儘管6個月年化口徑略降至45%，然短期通膨動能仍有回升跡象，加上10月PPI年增率升至6.5%，以及近期Fed官員釋出
進一步緊縮訊號，市場已提高12月升息及2027年初持續升息的預期，利率維持高檔的時間可能拉長。

• 市場看法：在通膨黏著性與升息預期同步升高下，美債殖利率及美元可能維持相對強勢，長天期債券與高評價成長股面
臨較大壓力；債券建議優先配置短天期美債、浮動利率債及高信評投資等級債，股票則以獲利穩健、現金流充足及具定
價能力的企業為核心，藉以降低因市場波動而衍生的風險。
10月核心PCE動能料反彈
資料來源：BEA, BLS, Bloomberg Economics，2026.11.17。
華許關注的PCE通膨指標10月可能未現改善

升息預期升溫、緊縮效應浮現，美債布局契機逐步醞釀
• 升息預期升溫，殖利率走高提升債券收益吸引力：Fed官員近
期密集釋出偏鷹訊號，10月FOMC會議紀錄亦顯示，多數官員
認為若通膨未能有效降溫，仍有必要進一步升息，部分官員甚
至認為不排除採取更大幅度的升息措施，市場因此提高對12月
升息的預期，10年期美債殖利率於11月中旬升至5.1%左右，
反映市場對利率維持高檔及進一步緊縮的預期；惟從債券投資
角度來看，殖利率走高也同步提升新投入資金的收益水準，若
後續通膨壓力逐步緩解，將有機會為債券價格回升創造條件。
• 緊縮政策壓抑需求，經濟降溫有望帶動債市轉機：美國經濟目
前仍受AI相關資本支出及企業投資支撐，但高利率環境將逐步
提高企業融資成本、壓抑消費與投資動能，若經濟成長因此放

緩，並帶動通膨壓力降溫，市場對未來政策利率的預期便可能
由升息轉向持平甚至下修，進而推動美債殖利率回落、債券價
格回升。尤其當經濟降溫疑慮升高時，美國公債的避險需求亦
可能增加，為短中天期債券帶來布局機會。
• 市場看法：後續應持續關注12月FOMC會議決策、通膨及就業
數據，以及升息對企業融資與民間需求的影響。建議採取穩健
防禦、分批布局策略，優先留意短中天期美國公債及投資等級
債，在殖利率相對高檔時掌握較具吸引力的收益水準；待通膨
降溫、實質殖利率回落及降息預期逐步形成，再視市場變化適
度調整存續期間，以兼顧債息收益與後續資本利得機會。

中美科技脫鉤不可能逆轉
美中科技戰升級，中國半導體自主化進程加速
• 美中科技戰升級，中國半導體自主化進程加速：輝達原本向中國銷售H200晶片，由黃仁勳與川普協商促成，根據該協
議，晶片必須經由美國運送，讓美國政府可以收取25%的關稅，近日美國公布收緊對中國AI晶片出口限制，輝達也停止
向中國銷售H200晶片，顯示美中科技戰進一步升級，與此同時，中國其實早已積極推動半導體自主化，降低對美國技
術的依賴，隨著美國封堵海外資料中心提供中國AI算力的管道，中國勢必加速採用國產替代方案，華為有望成為最大受
益者，華為昇騰系列AI處理器是輝達硬體的替代選擇。
• 展望後市：H200晶片的出口管制反覆政策變化，促使中國企業加速開發本土替代產品，加上中國經濟正從房地產開發，
轉向高科技帶動經濟成長，彭博預估2026年中國高科技產業的規模將達到27兆元人民幣，占GDP的18.3%。總結來說，

美中科技戰將使全球AI 供應鏈逐漸形成兩套體系，以美國為核心，另一套以中國自主晶片為核心，長期而言這將改變
半導體、AI 伺服器及資料中心產業的競爭格局。

地產銷售與開工增速仍處低位
商品房銷售面積 房屋新開工面積 房屋竣工面積
房地產銷售和開工偏弱，開發投資承壓
• 房地產銷售和開工偏弱，開發投資承壓：2026年前三季中國房地產市場仍處於調整階段，整體復甦力道不足，1至9月房
地產開發投資年減20.6%，新建商品房銷售面積年減13.2%，顯示建商投資與購屋需求依然疲弱，雖然部分核心城市房市
出現改善跡象，但尚未有效帶動全國市場回溫，預估第四季局部市場的好轉，對全國開發投資的提振作用有限，從交易
結構來看，新房與二手房市場持續分化，9月新房銷售面積降幅較前期擴大，而二手房成交面積則年增10.7%，顯示購屋
需求轉為價格較具優勢的二手市場，價格方面，一線城市二手住宅價格9月較上月上漲，新房與二手房價格維持正成長，
但漲幅逐漸收斂，但是二、三線城市房價仍呈波動整理，尚未形成明顯回升趨勢。

• 展望後市：中國房地產開發投資累計年增率降幅，可望較2026年進一步收斂，房市銷售回暖不代表投資立即回升，當成
交改善後，房企往往優先將資金用於提升現金流、推進在建工程及履行交屋責任，以降低財務壓力並穩定營運，唯有當
住宅去化速度持續，因此市場預估2027年房地產市場將延續築底，整體呈現「銷售先穩、投資後升」的溫和修復格局。

Semi Analysis下調AI算力資本開支
• Semi Analysis下修AI資本支出預測：半導體研究機構Semi Analysis於11月13日更新AI產業模型，下修未來數年全球AI加
速器出貨、資本支出、HBM需求及電力需求預測，其中2028至2030年調整幅度最為明顯。全球AI加速器資本支出預測分
別下調28%、41%及40%，2030年預測值由原先的1.531兆美元降至9,230億美元。
• AI收入增速放緩，融資環境成為主要壓力：此次下修主要反映AI商業化進展與資金供給不如預期。Semi Analysis指出，前
沿AI實驗室第三季新增年度經常性收入（ARR）增速放緩，使市場重新評估未來Token收入能否持續支撐龐大的算力成本；
另一方面，債權與股權投資者對AI基礎設施的融資意願降低，尤其缺乏大型雲端服務商長期採購承諾的專案，面臨較高的

建設與資金風險。此外，美國中期選舉後，資料中心監管及審批不確定性增加，預計部分原訂2027年下半年至2028年上
半年投產的資料中心將延後。不過，Semi Analysis認為，AI收入成長放緩與融資環境收緊，才是本次預測下修的主要原因。

硬體需求同步下修，供應鏈回歸正常化
• 硬體需求同步下修，供應鏈回歸正常化：除資本支出外，Semi Analysis亦下調AI硬體需求預測，2026至2030年全球AI加
速器邏輯晶片出貨面積累計下修17%；HBM方面，2028年出貨量預測由9.0EB降至6.4EB，下修30%，反映供需逐步改善
及價格漲勢趨緩。此外，2030年AI加速器功耗需求預測由122.8GW降至80.7GW，下修34%。整體而言，AI硬體需求仍
持續成長，但先前由晶片短缺、記憶體漲價及高額資本支出推動的擴張模式，正逐步回歸較合理的供需水準。
• AI產業進入效率競爭，資本支出仍具成長空間：整體而言，Semi Analysis此次下修並不代表AI需求消失，而是市場開始
重新檢視AI投資的商業回報、融資可持續性及資料中心建置效率。未來AI產業發展將不再單純追求算力規模擴張，而是更

重視運算成本、能源效率與投資報酬率。儘管AI基礎設施資本支出仍將維持成長，但成長速度可能低於先前預期，且資金
將更集中於具備明確客戶需求、穩定融資來源及較佳成本效益的建置計畫。

AI晶片供應商：輝達面臨最大下修壓力
公司 Semi Analysis 主要看法 影響與評估
輝達 未來出貨量預測遭大幅下修；大型AI實驗室轉向其他運算平台 加上Neocloud客戶融資風險提高，新一代晶片亦面臨降價壓力 本次下修幅度最大 未來營收與毛利率可能同時承壓
博通 2026至2030年ASIC業務累計營收預測下修11% 主要反映HBM成本下降帶動售價調整，而非大幅削減出貨量 相對具韌性 受惠Google TPU及大型AI客戶自研ASIC需求，性能成本比與能效具優勢
AMD 2028至2030年出貨及資本支出預測遭下修 受惠Meta、OpenAI及Anthropic等大型客戶布局，部分新產品部署時程提前 相較輝達具一定韌性 但中長期仍面臨AI需求及資本支出下修風險

雲端平台與AI模型業者：自研晶片及運算成本成為關鍵
公司 Semi Analysis 主要看法 影響與評估
亞馬遜 Trainium在2027及2028年的出貨預測未遭下修，主因資料中心專案儲備充足、 晶片製造成本較低，且願意透過策略性補貼支持重要客戶 少數維持近端成長預期的平台，自研ASIC有助降低對外部GPU的依賴
Google TPU具備性能成本比及能源效率優勢 是大型AI實驗室採購的重要替代方案 自研ASIC有助降低運算成本，提升AI基礎設施競爭力
OpenAI 除持續使用GPU，也將AMD GPU及自研ASIC納入運算平台布局 算力來源逐步多元化，有助降低對單一GPU供應商的依賴
Anthropic 採用TPU、Trainium、AMD GPU及輝達GPU等四大運算平台；從性能成本 比考量，輝達GPU相對處於較不具優勢的位置 有利於具備成本及能效優勢的ASIC，替代GPU平台
Meta 與AMD透過認股權證安排建立合作關係，並布局客製化AI晶片 有助AMD取得大型客戶訂單，大型科技公司積極推動算力來源多元化

經理人經濟軟著陸成主流共識
• 根據11月份美銀美林經理人調查報告顯示經濟軟著陸成主流共識，過去引領全球股市狂飆的人工智慧(AI)題材正進
入壓力測試期，資金開始從高度集中的科技股撤出，轉向具備防禦性與合理估值的板塊。
• AI資本支出受檢視，科技股減碼創20年之最：過去市場無條件追捧AI概念股的風氣已經改變，法人機構開始用放大
鏡檢視企業的變現能力與財務體質，AI投資從擴張轉向檢視，高達淨38%的經理人認為目前企業在AI領域投資過度，
創下調查歷史新高；更有52%經理人預期未來半年內將有超大規模雲端服務商宣布削減資本支出。同時，更希望企
業將現金流用於改善資產負債表(42%)或增加股東回饋(33%)，僅16%支持繼續擴大資本支出。
• 尾端風險和最擁擠交易：AI泡沫(34%)與AI超大規模雲端服務商信用事件(31%)包辦市場前兩大尾端風險；此外，

38%經理人擔憂大型雲端商發行的債券可能成為系統性信用風險來源。作多全球半導體雖仍是最擁擠交易，但認同
度已從7月高峰的82%大幅滑落至39%，撤出的資金明顯轉向防禦型的醫療類股(淨加碼升至34%，躍居最受青睞產
業)、債券(減碼幅度由淨37%收斂至淨23%)以及消費類股。展望2027年，經理人最看好的資產類別依序為國際股票
(36%)、美股(24%)與黃金(17%)。
• 小結及建議：整體而言，在經濟溫和軟著陸背景下，投資策略建議多元配置、股債兼備以抵抗波動。
資料來源：美銀美林證券，富蘭克林投顧整理，2026.11

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選舉進入
最後衝刺階段 
經濟 
⚫ 美國第三季實質 GDP 季增年率(初值)由上季的 2.2%升至 3.4%，成長主
要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回補帶動。其中個人消費貢獻 2.52 個百
分點、私人投資貢獻 3.27 個百分點，惟淨出口拖累 3.32 個百分點，整
體經濟仍維持穩健擴張。 
⚫ 9 月美國新屋銷售年化 67.6 萬戶，月減 0.6%、年減 5.3%，主要是中西
部及南部地區銷售下滑拖累整體表現。房價雖較上月回升，但仍低於去
年同期；供給月數 8.4 個月，顯示市場仍呈供給充裕、需求偏弱格局。 
⚫ 美國 9 月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數年增率由 3.4%升至 3.6%，核心
PCE 物價指數年增率亦由 3.0%升至 3.1%，通膨再度升溫。儘管實質所
得受物價上漲影響而下滑，消費支出仍持續成長，反映家庭需求及內需
動能仍具韌性。 
⚫ 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 決議將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%
至 4.0%。決策聲明指出，美國經濟活動表現強勁，就業市場變化不大，
通膨持續高於 2%目標，將依後續數據評估適當政策立場，以確保通膨
回歸目標。兩名委員主張升息 1 碼，顯示內部對通膨風險仍存分歧。主
席 Kevin Warsh 強調將防止能源價格上漲擴散至整體物價，並宣布 2027
年起部分 FOMC 會議改為策略討論會議，以強化長期政策研議。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 26 日路透社民調顯示，川普施政支持率 32%，略高於前週；國會
選舉政黨支持度則由民主黨 47%領先共和黨 36%。截至 10 月 31 日，
提前及郵寄投票已逾 5,100 萬人。整體而言，期中選舉結果將影響未來
國會版圖、施政推動及貿易政策方向，備受市場關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國第三季 GDP 成長優於前季，主要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回
補帶動，顯示內需與企業活動仍具韌性；惟新屋銷售持續疲弱、房市需
求未明顯改善，反映高利率環境仍抑制部分經濟活動。另一方面，PCE
通膨再度升溫，核心通膨降幅有限，顯示物價壓力仍存。在經濟穩健且
通膨高於目標情況下，Fed 維持利率不變並釋出偏鷹訊號，後續貨幣政
策料將維持審慎，持續關注通膨與就業數據變化。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.11.2 
一、 美國—經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選
舉進入最後衝刺階段 
2026 年第三季美國實質國內生產毛額(GDP)季增年率初值為 3.4%，高於第二
季之 2.2%，整體景氣持續穩健擴張。就成長貢獻而言，個人消費支出仍為經濟成
長主要支柱，貢獻 2.52 個百分點，其中服務消費及商品消費分別貢獻 1.89 個百
分點及 0.63 個百分點，反映民間需求維持穩健；私人投資則貢獻 3.27 個百分點，
為本季成長最大亮點，其中固定投資貢獻 1.66 個百分點，而企業庫存回補亦額外
貢獻 1.61 個百分點。對外貿易方面，淨出口拖累經濟成長 3.32 個百分點，其中
出口減少造成 0.68 個百分點負面影響；進口增加則拖累 2.64 個百分點。政府消
費支出與投資則受聯邦政府非國防支出增加帶動，貢獻 0.90 個百分點。此外，第
三季個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數及核心 PCE 物價指數分別年增 2.1%及 2.5%，
均低於第二季水準。整體而言，美國第三季經濟成長主要受民間消費、企業投資
及庫存回補支撐，雖貿易逆差擴大對 GDP 形成明顯拖累，但內需表現仍具韌性，
顯示美國經濟基本面維持穩健。 
2026 年 9 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整後年化戶數為 67.6 萬戶，較上月減
少 0.6%，並較去年同期下降 5.3%，顯示在高利率環境下住宅需求仍較去年疲弱。
地區別表現分歧，其中東北部及西部分別較上月增加 28.0%及 21.4%，惟中西部
及南部分別減少 7.3%及 6.6%，尤其占全美銷售比重最大的南部地區回落，成為
拖累整體銷售的主因。價格方面，9 月新屋銷售中位價為 40.04 萬美元，較上月
上升 3.9%，但較去年同期下跌 3.9%，儘管房價高於上月，惟仍面臨修正壓力。
供給方面，9 月底待售新屋數量為 47.6 萬戶，較上月減少 1.0%，較去年同期減少
2.3%；按目前銷售速度計算，供給月數為 8.4 個月，低於上月的 8.5 個月，但高
於去年同期的 8.2 個月。整體而言，9 月美國新屋銷售較前月小幅下滑且年減幅
度擴大，反映購屋需求仍未明顯改善；雖然部分地區銷售回升且庫存略有下降，
但供給月數仍維持相對偏高水準，顯示新屋市場仍處於供給較充裕、需求偏弱的
格局，後續仍須觀察利率走勢及就業市場變化對住宅需求的影響。

準會 2%目標之過程仍具挑戰，後續仍須持續關注能源價格變化、核心通膨走勢
及消費動能對聯準會貨幣政策方向之影響。 
聯準會(Fed)於 2026 年 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 表決結果，決定將聯邦基
金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，符合市場預期；謹將本次會議聲明
及主席 Kevin Warsh 記者會重點摘錄如下：(一)聯準會認為美國經濟活動持續以
強勁步調擴張，較前次聲明所稱之「穩健擴張」進一步上修對經濟表現之評價；
儘管地緣政治情勢使經濟前景不確定性仍偏高，但國內支出維持韌性，生產力成
長與資本投資表現強勁，就業增幅大致與勞動力成長同步，失業率變動不大，顯
示整體經濟基本面仍具相當韌性；(二)通膨仍高於聯準會 2%目標，委員會表示需
累積更多經濟與物價資訊，以評估適當政策立場，確保通膨適時回歸 2%目標，
並重申將致力維持物價穩定；(三)本次決議出現兩張反對票，聯準會理事 Lisa Cook
及克里夫蘭聯邦準備銀行總裁 Beth Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，反映部分委員對通
膨風險仍抱持較為謹慎態度；(四)主席 Kevin Warsh 於記者會表示，近期聯邦公
開市場委員會討論焦點多集中於物價穩定，特別是在近期能源及相關價格再度上
揚情況下，聯準會有責任避免個別產業價格上漲擴散至整體經濟，並確保通膨預
期維持穩定；(五)Warsh 並宣布調整 FOMC 會議安排，2027 年仍將維持每年 8 次
會議，但其中 1 次將改為專門討論貨幣政策策略之會議，不進行利率表決或發布
政策聲明，並預期 2028 年將有 2 次會議採取此模式，以騰出更多時間進行長期
政策與重大議題研議。整體而言，本次會議雖維持利率不變，但聯準會仍認為通
膨問題尚未完全解決，後續政策方向仍將取決於通膨、勞動市場及整體經濟數據
表現。

二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%，但有部份是受到愛爾蘭經濟成長率大幅
波動的扭曲，若剔除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率僅約 0.2%。該季受到
物價壓力上升、地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響，對消費及投資需求構成負面因素，
但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使歐元區經濟保持正向表現。就國
別而言，德、法、義、西 GDP 季增率分別為 1.2%、-0.1%、0.8%、2.7%；其中德
國經濟活動受到財政擴張的提振；法國的消費支出雖強勁，但受到投資減緩和進
口增加拖累；義大利製造業產出萎縮拖累成長表現；西班牙在住宅營建熱潮的支
持下，經濟活動持續優於其他國家，但歐盟 NGEU 計畫到期以及政府財政整頓力
道，西班牙後續經濟動能可能會逐漸放緩。 
 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變，主要是近期歐元區各國殖利
率普遍上升，對經濟造成限制性因素，且不確定性可能會隨著時間的推移而累積，
從而拖累經濟成長。不過，歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相
當大，使央行考慮進一步採取貨幣政策措施因應。因此，除非經濟成長受到超出
預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的
政策會議將會宣布升息一碼。 
三、日本—日本央行暫停升息，通膨壓力續存，升息預期升溫 
 9 月工業生產月增 2.2%，扭轉 8 月受天災造成生產中斷而下滑的情勢，從細
項來看，汽車、電子產品及生產機械產量均呈回升，其中生產機械已連續第四個
月成長，反映 AI 相關資本支出需求持續暢旺；庫存月增 2.5%，石化產品庫存亦
逐步回補。展望未來，製造商預估 10 月及 11 月產量將分別月增 5.1%及 0.8%，

侵蝕購買力，為消費復甦的下行風險。 
 10 月東京核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，石油相關成本上漲已由核心商品逐步擴散至服
務價格，食品及生鮮食品價格亦偏強。雖目前能源、汽油補貼等政府措施仍抑制
通膨，但隨 12 月相關補貼逐步退場，成本壓力將進一步反映於商品及服務價格，
預期至 2026 年底潛在通膨仍可能高於央行的 2%目標，通膨黏性值得持續關注。 
 日本央行於 10 月 29、30 日召開貨幣政策會議，維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，
主要係仍需觀察先前升息的累積效果，但整體政策訊號仍偏鷹派。本次決議以 8：
1 通過，唯一反對的政策委員高田創主張再升息 1 碼至 1.5%，反映央行內部對通
膨上行風險仍具警戒。日本央行指出通膨已大致達到與 2%通膨目標相符的水準，
同時將 2027 財年的核心 CPI(不含生鮮食品)預測由 2.4%上調至 2.6%，顯示目前
通膨情勢仍支持貨幣政策進一步正常化。央行總裁表示，目前金融環境仍屬寬鬆，
未來若經濟及物價走勢符合預期，仍將適時調升政策利率，市場因而持續關注後
續會議再度升息的可能性。此外，日本央行亦決定自 2027 年 1 月起，將 ETF 出
售規模由約 3,300 億日圓提高至 8,600 億日圓，加速資產負債表正常化進程。 
四、中國—工業部門成本轉嫁能力有限，使利潤率持續萎縮

五、印度及東南亞—泰、菲進出口創歷史新高，泰國央行按兵不動以支撐經濟 
(1) 新加坡：9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率小幅回落至 2.2%、核心通膨率自 8
月的 2.2%降至 2%，反映交通運輸（7.4%）、服裝鞋帽（2.1%）及家庭耐用品
和服務（0.2%）價格漲幅顯著放緩，此外，由於行業競爭壓力，資訊通信技術
服務價格的進一步回落也抑制整體服務價格的上漲。隨著 11 月電力和天然氣
價格下調生效，預計 2026 年第四季核心通膨將進一步放緩。 
(2) 泰國：9 月出口額達 384.4 億美元、年增 24.0%，創歷史新高，主要得益於工
業產品出口成長 25.4%，其中，以電腦及相關設備（59.4%）、電話及零件
（117.5%）及電路板（45.9%）成長最為顯著，對美國出口額年增 50.1%，對
中國和日本的出口額分別成長 18.4%和 19.0%。9 月進口額 443 億美元、年增
50.4%，創歷史新高，因出口導向製造業生產需求及全球能源價格的上漲，主
要類別進口額均有所成長，其中，資本貨物(電腦、機械零件、電路板等)進口
大增 92.3%，燃料、原材料和中間產品進口增幅均逾 4 成、運輸設備在飛機與
航空設備進口大增 12 倍下成長 24%。9 月貿易逆差大幅擴大至 59 億美元，累
計 1 至 9 月，貿易逆差達 437 億美元，出口額成長 19.6%、進口額成長 37.8%。
泰國央行 10 月會議維持基準利率於 1%不變，維持寬鬆的貨幣政策立場，以
支持經濟復甦的不平衡。經濟預估方面，強於預期的出口以及與科技和人工智
慧週期相關的投資，預估 2026 年和 2027 年經濟成長率將分別成長 2.4%和
2.1%，然而，高額的家庭債務、脆弱的勞動市場和疲軟的購買力繼續抑制私人
消費，使經濟成長仍低於潛在水平。通膨方面，受能源價格上漲和聖嬰現象的
影響，預計通膨率在 2027 年初將出現短暫上升，之後隨著國內需求的疲軟而
回落，2026 年和 2027 年整體通膨率平均分別為 2.1%和 1.3%。

進口額成長 20.3%。 
六、台灣—AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健 
9 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較上月增加 1 分，燈號
續呈紅燈，其中工業及服務業加班工時，受惠 AI 相關產業生產動能維持熱絡，
由綠燈轉呈黃紅燈，其餘八項燈號均維持不變。另領先與同時指標皆呈上升，顯
示國內經濟動能維持穩健。 
主計總處 10 月 30 日公布台灣第三季經濟成長率為 14.36%，主要受 AI 需求
強勁帶動出口及企業投資大幅成長，民間消費亦維持熱絡，顯示外需動能正逐步
向內需擴散，整體經濟呈現內外皆熱。展望未來，AI 需求、半導體相關投資及企
業資本支出持續擴張，加上年底消費旺季，可望支撐短期景氣表現；惟今年經濟
高速成長墊高比較基期，明年增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成
長動能向傳統產業擴散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

## Document 088: 2026年7～9月期のGDP（1次速報）結果

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7～9月期のGDP（1次速報）結果.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1 / 2

2026 年 11 月 17 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）結果

～個人消費が力強さを欠き、4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表した 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比－0.0%（年率換算   
－0.1%）と減少した。設備投資が前期比＋1.6%と高めの伸びとなった一方、個人消費が同－0.3%と 2 四半期連
続で減少したことなどが下押し要因となり、小幅ながら 4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。なお、輸入が鉱物
性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は－0.6%ポイントと大きめのマイナスとなったが、公的在庫の寄与
度が＋0.5%ポイントとなり、おおむね相殺された。 
過去 3 四半期にわたって潜在成長率を上回る高めの伸びが続いていたことや、設備投資が堅調に増加したこ
とを踏まえると、今回のマイナス成長をもって景気回復の動きが途切れたとは判断できないが、景気が力強さを欠
いていることも事実だろう。特に GDP の過半を占める個人消費に弱さがみられたことは懸念材料である。今後は、
良好な雇用・所得環境に支えられて個人消費は持ち直しへ向かうと期待されるが、消費者物価の伸び拡大や住
宅ローン金利の上昇による家計負担の増加を通じて、消費の減少が続くリスクには警戒が必要である。 
なお、物価上昇分を含んだ名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.4%（年率換算＋1.6%）と、10 四半期連続のプラス成長
となった。物価上昇圧力は依然として強く、コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比  
＋2.4%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.4%）と、高い伸びが続いた。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度（%ポイント）

### Page 2

2 / 2

 1 次速報値を踏まえた、2026 年 7～9 月期における需要項目別の動向（実質ベース）は以下の通り。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－0.3%） 
 家計を取り巻く所得環境は改善した（実質雇用者報酬は前期比＋0.3%）が、前期に自動車や
エアコンが一時的な要因によって増加した反動で耐久財が前期比－3.5%と減少した上、夏場
の地震や台風、長雨などの自然災害・天候不順によって消費の機会に恵まれなかったこと、食
料品の値上げや住宅ローン金利の上昇など今後の生活不安につながる話題も多く節約志向が
強まったことから、半耐久財や非耐久財も減少した。ただし、サービスは 9 月のシルバーウイー
クの日並びが良かったこともあり、小幅に増加した。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－1.1%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数が 2 四半期ぶりに減少する中、建築コストの増加も逆風とな
り、住宅投資は 2 四半期連続で減少した。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比＋1.6%） 
 企業の省力化や生産性向上に向けた投資意欲は強く、特に 7～9 月期はサーバーや半導体製
造装置といった AI 関連投資も好調だったことで、設備投資は 3 四半期ぶりに増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度＋0.0%ポイント） 
 前期に大きめのプラス寄与となったこともあり、民間在庫の寄与は横ばいとなった。 
 なお、公的在庫は国家備蓄原油の減少幅が縮小したことから、前期比寄与度＋0.5%ポイントと
なった。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費（前期比－0.0%） 
 前期に高めの伸びとなったこともあり、横ばいだった。 
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－1.1%） 
 名目では増加したものの、工事コストの増加を受けたデフレーターの上昇に追いついておらず、
実質では 2 四半期連続で減少した。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度－0.6%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比－0.2%、実質輸入：同＋2.5%） 
 輸出は前期比－0.2%と減少した。財は堅調な米国向けや中東向けの持ち直しを受けて増加し
た一方、サービスは前期に研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れた反動で減少した。 
 輸入は前期比＋2.5%と増加した。特に財が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直した。ただし、サービス
については減少した。 
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は－0.6%ポイントと大きめのマイナスになった。

## Document 118: 261112_citi_msi

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_citi_msi.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins from ASP Pass-Through and Richer Mix
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), 
beating Citi/cons by 27%/45% on stronger-than-expected margins and 
non-op gains. GPM of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8 ppts YoY) topped Citi/cons 
at 15.8%/14.5%, as ASP hikes passed through rising component costs while 
moving-avg inventory costs lagged, alongside a richer high-end product 
mix. For 4Q26E, we expect further ASP increases and a rising server 
contribution to largely offset seasonal shipment declines. Into 2027, we 
expect PC demand to stay weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases moderately. We raise 2026E-28E earnings and our 
TP to NT$145 (10x 2027E EPS). We stay Neutral as the share price already 
reflects the pass-through upside.
3Q26: Margin-led beat on cost pass-through and better mix — MSI reported 
3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), beating Citi/cons forecasts 
by 27%/45%, mainly on stronger-than-expected margins and non-op gains. GPM 
of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8ppts YoY) came in above Citi/cons at 15.8%/14.5%. 
Mgmt. attributed the upside to 1) ASP increase, as MSI raised prices in line with 
suppliers’ cost increases while its moving-average inventory costs rose less; and 2) 
a higher mix of high-end products. Non-op gains, including FX gains of over 
NT$100mn, further boosted the bottom line.
Price hikes to continue; 2027 PC market to keep shrinking — We reiterate our view 
that 4Q26 shipments will decline seasonally from the 3Q26 peak. However, mgmt. 
expects rising raw material costs and continued pass-through to keep lifting ASPs, 
offsetting lower volumes and making the seasonal decline milder than in prior years. 
The server business should also contribute more, potentially reaching a high-single-
digit revenue share. Into 2027, we expect weak PC demand amid elevated costs, 
with MSI’s MB and PC shipments declining by over 10%. Pass-through room should 
narrow as memory price increases moderately, though margins should stay elevated 
for a while, as mgmt. expects memory supply to stay tight in the coming quarters.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 6-27%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through and higher margin assumptions. 
We adjust TP to NT$145 (from NT$140), implying 10x 2027E EPS (from 12x). We 
believe the share price already reflects MSI’s opportunity to pass component costs 
through to end products, and we expect limited upside going forward, given that the 
PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain. Maintain Neutral.

Micro-Star (2377.TW)

Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$145 is based on 10x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 119: 261112_daiwa_accton

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_daiwa_accton.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review Accton’s 3Q26 results, released on 12 November.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat/miss expectations. Accton’s 3Q26 
net profit came in at TWD13.1bn (EPS: TWD23.3), in line with our estimate 
(TWD13.0bn) but 6% lower than the Bloomberg consensus (TWD13.9bn).  
Revenue was TWD120.7bn (+26% QoQ, 66% YoY), higher than our/BBG 
estimates by 13%/2%, mainly due to increasing AI card contribution for 
Trainium 3. Nonetheless, gross margin fell by 2.1pp QoQ to 17.6% (vs. our 
19.1% estimate), reflecting rising material costs (especially from memory). 
Consequently, OPM declined by 1.6pp to 13.4% (vs. our 15.1% estimate), 
which was slightly offset by better operating leverage.

Business outlook. We believe gross margin is likely the key highlight of 
the 3Q26 results. Per management, gross margin was diluted due to rising 
memory costs. As such, Accton has shifted its business model from “Buy-&-
Sell” to consignment from September. Since then, we see gross margin 
gradually recovering back to the 20% level. Looking into 4Q26, we believe 
Accton could sustain the gross margin, but the networking chipset shortage 
might weigh on revenue momentum in the short term. The alternative is 
that one of the key switch clients could shift its orders slightly towards 
Spectrum first to ease the near-term shortage issue, in our view. As for the 
AI card business, we believe the total shipment has been largely stable 
since 3Q26. Even when the end-client gradually shifts its product mix from 
air-cooled to the liquid-cooled version, we believe the rising content from 
the liquid-cooled version should be able to offset the slightly lower ASP per 
chipset given there are more chipsets (4x vs. 2x from air-cooled) on an 
ASIC AI motherboard. Looking into 2027, we maintain our original view that 
total AMZN ASIC units will grow c.30% YoY to c.3m as the first growth 
drivers. On top of this, the liquid-cooled version has adopted the PCIE 
scale-up switch, which can expand TAM for Accton, in our view. Lastly, for 
the top-of-rack (ToR) switches, we expect 800G switches to remain the 
mainstream models in 2027 but with rising enterprise usage, which could 
likely be the next growth driver in 2027.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 6-12% to factor in 
higher ToR switch revenue. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating, but lower our 
12M TP to TWD3,000 (prior: TWD3,666), based on a target PER multiple of 
24x (prior: 35x, within its past-3-year range of 10-33x), applied to our 1-
year forward EPS forecast, to reflect the weaker market sentiment. Key 
downside risk: lower-than-expected ASIC server and switch demand.

How we differ: Our 2027E EPS is 5% higher than the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more positive view on operating leverage.

12 November 2026

3Q26 review: gross margin dilution on memory costs

➢ Fundamentals largely on track with our prior estimates  
➢ Recent pullbacks provide good entry points to build positions 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; lowering 12M TP to TWD3,000

Company profile 
Founded in 1988, Accton is dedicated to the design and manufacturing of enterprise networking and 
communication products. With its long-term experience and expertise, Accton is one of the leading 
open-source networking ODM partners for major players worldwide, with particular focuses on data 
centres, telco carriers and campus networks. Major products include enterprise switches, metro access 
switches, network applications and wireless products.

### Page 5

5

Accton Technology (2345 TT): 12 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 12 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Accton’s board has 7 directors, with 3 independent directors. The independent directors account
for 43% of all directors. A total of 8 board meetings were held in 2025, and the average actual
attendance rate was 95%.
The backgrounds of independent directors are diversified and their competence includes
business management, accounting, finance, and work experience in the medical industry. We
deem the diversified background as a positive factor for the company’s board quality.
Besides, the Chairman and President are not the same person, which increases the board and
executive management’s quality. Based on its board structure and key executive hiring, we
believe that Accton has already professionalised the family business by allowing external
professionals to run the business for major shareholders.
Capital management 1
Accton declared DPS of TWD15.0 in 2025, representing a 31.8% payout ratio. The ratio was
lower than the 51.2% in 2024 and its historical range of 69-75% from 2018 to 2022. That said, it
still points towards a friendly shareholder dividend policy, in our view.
We attribute the lower payout ratio mainly to the sharp earnings growth and continued capacity
investments in Taiwan and Vietnam amid US-China tensions. The DPS CAGR in the past 10
years was c.23%, which showcased Accton’s capability in capital management. The continuous
improvement in networking capabilities (eg, 400G and 800G switches), alongside expansion into
AI accelerator modules, has helped it differentiate itself from other competitors and strengthen its
position in the white-box switch supply chain for major US cloud service providers.
Related party &
transaction 1
In 2025, revenue from related parties accounted for 0.15% of total revenue, while purchases
from related parties accounted for 2.0% of cost of sales. We see limited risk from related-party
transactions.
S Supply chain management 1
Like most vendors, Accton asks its suppliers to follow: 1) the hazard substance requirements of
the RoHS, Reach and corporate social responsibilities (5 categories, including labour, health and
safety, environmental, ethics and management system) required by the RBA Code of Conduct,
and 2) QC08000 (Hazardous Substances Process Management), while the company conducts
annual reviews to evaluate its suppliers’ performance.
To effectively track, communicate and manage its supply chain to manufacture green products,
each supplier can use the eGreen system® platform, developed independently by Accton, to
learn about environmental protection control and management requirements for components and
materials.
S Data security 2
To ensure information security for customers and the company, Accton obtained ISO/IEC
27001:2013 certification in 2016 and completed the transition to ISO/IEC 27001:2022 in 2024,
with continued verification of its information security policies and controls in 2025. Apart from the
global information security standards, Accton also takes measures to improve its information
security, including:1) Implementing business continuity and disaster recovery mechanisms,
including annual recovery drills for critical systems and databases. In 2025, the company
achieved its target of recovering critical systems within 48 hours. 2) Conducting monthly
vulnerability scans across company-wide servers, network equipment and core systems to
identify potential security risks. Accton also conducts quarterly social engineering drills to
strengthen employee awareness of cybersecurity threats.
In 2025, Accton conducted four phishing simulation exercises involving over 13,000 participant
attendances, achieving an average phishing safe rate of 93%. The company reported no major
information security incidents or personal data breaches during the year.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

M
Update
Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Component supply to improve: While component supply could stay tight near
term, Delta expects to secure bigger volume in the coming quarters, so it should
have better order fulfillment ahead. Oct-26 monthly sales seems to point to double-
digit% QoQ revenue growth in 4Q26, in our view, and it is likely to see better than
seasonal growth in 1Q27. It expects the component supply tightness to start easing

in 2Q27.
HVDC contribution minor  near term: It should start to ship HVDC power racks in
4Q26, but the contribution should be relatively small. For the GPU platform,
scheduled for 2H27, Delta sees 80VDC as the only reference design as of now,
although it does not seem to be finalized yet. Our conversations indicate most
investors do not have that as the base case, so there could be upside to consensus
estimates in 2028, if that ends up being the case.

Fuel cell another driver longer term: Delta will have some pilot production in late
2026. Its BoD has approved a greenfield project for the fuel cell business. This fits
well into its power delivery business, especially amid the power constraints that
hyperscalers are being confronted with now.

the next 12-18 months.

universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices stayed flat MoM in November: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices all  stayed flat for the month of November, consistent with our
expectations. Though major panel makers were able to raise panel prices in late

3Q26 to mitigate margin pressure from elevated component costs, output control
measures aimed at enhancing bargaining power have been undermined by tapering
order momentum heading into the slow season. This is typical of the situation in the
past few years, where demand is typically the more important factor driving up
panel prices and supply side discipline is more about protecting prices on the
downside. Our base case assumption remains that TV panel prices should stay

roughly flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average basis, though our checks indicate
that some large TV panel models might see prices tick up in December.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in November: Monitor and notebook panel
prices to stay flat MoM during the month. Similar to the dynamics in the TV space,
seasonality is keeping end demand muted, especially on the NB side, which weakens
panel makers' leverage in price negotiations. On a quarterly average basis, we expect

IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.
Glass in advanced packaging already in the valuation: Despite the recent pullback,
Taiwanese panel makers AUO (2409.TW) and Innolux (3481.TW) are now trading at
1.2x and 1.0x 2027 P/B – still above respective peaks of 1.1x and 0.9x since 2023.
Since glass in advanced packaging is  tied to the AI theme, we think such levels look
elevated compared to other AI stocks in Taiwan approaching trough valuations even

before this round of AI rally.  In China, we have a    relative preference for BOE
(000725.SZ; 1.1x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.8x P/B) for its scale, faster progress in
glass core substrates and cheaper valuation.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Price hike negotiations to continue in the coming months.
Current valuations of 1.0-1.8x P/B for panel makers should come down, along
with other AI-related names, especially in Taiwan.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trended Down from 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, while September, October and
November were flat MoM,   in line with our expectation that TV panel prices would start
trending down from 3Q26 onwards.  That said, we do not expect a significant decline as

supply-side discipline should provide downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expected the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup

in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events
materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expected the restocking momentum seen since the start
of the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  to Be at Low 80s% in 4Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%

in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26, to 85%. We believe utilization will

further edge down to a  low-80s% level in 4Q26 as the panel makers further tighten
output amid low season.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry will undergo
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can

remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also
support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, covered by Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT
of NT$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we appreciate AUO's
efforts to diversify its business, meaningful earnings contributions are likely to take
time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,

higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks
suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and

additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): The turnaround story is largely behind us, as
OLED has become the dominant earnings driver. While the quality of the business
has improved, we remain cautious on the sustainability of earnings expansion. The
stock trades at 0.5x 2026e P/B vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.

• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are Equal-weight with a PT of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.8x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,
gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers

in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/11/19 投資速報

電子–電腦及週邊設備 
優群 (3217.TT) 
短期利空逐步反映，營運落底迎接成長

買進(調升評等) 
出刊緣由：財報公佈

個股與大盤走勢

近一個月 近三個月 近半年 近一年 
加權指數報酬率 -14.9 -12.6 -2.6 46.1 
個股股價報酬率 -22.1 -24.7 -41.2 -34.4

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

優群 3Q26 獲利低於預期，主要受筆電需求疲弱、稼動率下降及原
物料成本上漲影響，預期 4Q26 營運仍將承壓。惟隨 Google 伺服
器新案及網通新產品於 2027 年放量，加上高單價新產品陸續導
入，營收與獲利可望重返成長。研究部下修優群 2026、2027 年稅
後 EPS 至 10.06 元（原估 11.31 元）及 11.83 元（原估 14.30 
元）。考量短期利空已逐步反映於股價，現價對應 2027 年本益比
僅 8.8 倍，低於歷史區間下緣，且預估殖利率約 7.6%，具備下檔
支撐及評價修復空間。研究部以 2027 年 EPS 為評價基礎，給予 
12 倍本益比，目標價 140 元，投資評等由中立調升至買進。

重點摘要： 
1. 筆電需求疲弱、稼動率下降，3Q26 獲利低於預期 
優群 3Q26 營收 9.75 億元（+2.9% QoQ、−13.8% YoY），低於原
估 10.90 億元，主因筆電客戶持續調整出貨，SO-DIMM 與 M.2 營
收分別年減 36% 及 20%，抵銷伺服器與新產品成長貢獻。受營收
不如預期、產能利用率下降及原物料成本上漲影響，毛利率降至 
46.1%（−1.5ppt QoQ、−5.1ppt YoY），低於原估 47.4%；營益率
降至 27.8%，低於原估 29.9%。稅後淨利 2.55 億元（+18.9% QoQ、
−32.8% YoY），稅後 EPS 2.84 元，低於原估 3.42 元。

表一、優群 3Q26 獲利檢討及 4Q26 獲利修正

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 筆電需求持續疲弱，下修 4Q26 獲利預估 
優群 10 月營收 2.58 億元（−22.9% MoM、−25.9% YoY），主要受
筆電客戶持續調整出貨影響。預期 11、12 月出貨將較 10 月回
升，惟整體需求仍偏弱，產能利用率下降將持續壓抑毛利率表現。
考量筆電需求修正幅度超出預期，研究部下修 4Q26 營收預估至 
8.80 億元（−9.8% QoQ、−15.5% YoY，原估 10.26 億元），毛利
率由原估 47.8% 下修至 45.4%，稅後 EPS 由 2.69 元下修至 
2.02 元（−29.3% QoQ、−37.9% YoY）。

3. CSP 伺服器與網通新產品放量，2027 年營運結構持續優化 
隨 AI 推論及企業運算需求成長，伺服器對記憶體容量、頻寬及傳
輸效能的要求持續提升，帶動 DDR5 R-DIMM 朝高速化與高容量發
展。相較傳統 U-DIMM，R-DIMM 對訊號完整性及可靠度要求較高，
連接器製程與品質門檻亦隨之提升，有利高單價產品需求成長。優
群持續拓展 Meta 與 Google 等 CSP 客戶訂單，2026 年 R-DIMM 
累計出貨量已超越 2025 年全年，且高單價 R-DIMM 占比持續提
升，有助優化產品組合。其中，Google 伺服器新案預計於 1Q27 在
越南廠量產，除帶動 Long-DIMM 出貨成長，亦有助提升越南廠稼
動率並改善規模經濟，研究部預估 2027 年 Long-DIMM 營收成長 
30.9% YoY。網通方面，優群持續拓展既有筆電與伺服器以外的通
訊設備應用，新產品已於 3Q26 開始量產，預期 2027 年隨客戶訂
單增加及新應用拓展，出貨規模持續擴大。搭配 CAMM2、CCBB 等
新產品貢獻，研究部預估 2027 年 Others 營收成長 33.8% YoY，
成為營運結構轉型的重要成長來源。相較之下，傳統筆電 SO-DIMM 
營收預估衰退 2.4% YoY，顯示營運成長動能逐步由筆電轉向伺服
器與網通應用，有助降低對筆電景氣的依賴。

圖一、優群產品營收比重（2024～2027F）

資料來源：優群，統一投顧預估

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. 記憶體模組化與可維修設計趨勢，帶動高單價產品滲透 
隨筆電朝輕薄化、高速傳輸及可維修設計發展，連接器規格持續升
級，帶動高單價產品導入。記憶體方面，傳統 SO-DIMM 受限於模
組高度與訊號傳輸需求，部分高階筆電轉向焊接式 LPDDR 設計，
壓抑傳統連接器需求；CAMM2 則兼具低矮化、高速傳輸及模組可更
換等優勢，提供新一代記憶體連接方案。優群 CAMM2 目前月出貨
約 5～10 萬顆，HP 約 10 款高階機種正進行認證，預計 1H27 量
產，隨模組供應改善及新機種導入，可望擴大出貨規模。Type-C 方
面，隨 USB-C 成為筆電充電與資料傳輸的主要介面，接口維修成
本逐漸受到品牌重視。優群推出可獨立拆卸的兩件式 Type-C，無
須因接口損壞而更換整片主機板，單價約為傳統產品的兩倍，目前
已導入聯想機種，HP 預計於 2027 年加入。此外，Metal Bar 微
型沖壓件持續由筆電拓展至記憶體及 SiP 模組，憑藉精密沖壓與
微型化製程能力，擴大高毛利產品應用。研究部預估 2027 年 
Metal Bar 營收成長約 18% YoY，隨高單價新產品滲透率提升，有
助優化產品組合並支撐毛利率回升。

5. 筆電需求修正超出預期，下修 2026 年獲利，2027 年重返成
長 
考量筆電需求疲弱、客戶持續調整出貨，加上稼動率下降及原物料
成本上漲壓抑獲利，研究部下修優群 2026 年營收預估至 37.50 
億元（−9.6% YoY，原估 40.11 億元），其中 SO-DIMM 與 M.2 營
收分別衰退 29% 及 17% YoY，為主要拖累；毛利率由原估 47.9% 
下修至 47.0%，稅後淨利預估 9.03 億元（−21.0% YoY），稅後 
EPS 由 11.31 元下修至 10.06 元。 
展望 2027 年，隨 CSP 伺服器新案放量、網通業務擴張及高單價
新產品持續貢獻，研究部預估 Long-DIMM、Metal Bar 及其他產品
營收分別成長約 31%、18% 及 34% YoY，帶動全年營收回升至 
41.12 億元（+9.7% YoY，原估 46.67 億元）。隨營收規模擴大、
稼動率提升及產品組合改善，毛利率預估回升至 47.6%，稅後淨利 
10.54 億元（+16.8% YoY），稅後 EPS 11.83 元（+16.8% YoY，
原估 14.30 元）。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

6. 短期利空已逐步反映，低評價與高殖利率浮現投資價值 
考量筆電需求疲弱及短期獲利下修，優群股價已大幅修正，現價對
應 2027 年本益比僅 8.8 倍，低於歷史 10～20 倍區間下緣，反
映短期營運利空已逐步消化。此外，優群近五年現金配息率約 78%
～79%，以研究部預估 2026 年 EPS 10.06 元推算，現金股利約 
7.9 元，按 11/18 收盤價 104.5 元計算，預估殖利率約 7.6%，
提供下檔支撐。展望 2027 年，隨 CSP 伺服器與網通新業務放量，
營收及獲利可望重返成長，帶動評價修復。綜合考量短期利空已逐
步反映、獲利成長動能回升及殖利率支撐，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 
為評價基礎，給予 12 倍本益比，目標價 140 元，投資評等由中
立調升至買進。

研 究 報 告                
大銀微系統(4576.TT) 
產業：電機機械 
受惠半導體設備擴充潮，2027 年營收成⻑將⾼於 20%  
 投資評價與建議 
高毛利率的奈微米定位平台營收比重已近60%，產品結構及附加價值提升帶動
毛利率提升，技術升級、集團分工與產能區域化佈局將帶動大銀微2027年營收
成⻑高於20%。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收年增77%，4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略⾼：亞洲(台灣除外)、
台灣、美洲及歐洲地區3Q26分別年增89%、101%、127%及-12%，全球半導
體供應鏈重組與在地化採購需求強勁，3Q26營收12.2億元季增19%(年增
77%)，高階定位平台出貨佔比提升及規模經濟發揮，毛利率42.4%季增
5.3pp(年增8.6pp)，規模經濟及費用控制得宜下，營業利益率24.3%季增
6.4pp(年增13.9pp)；稅前淨利3.3億元季增113%(年增255%)，EPS 2.15元，
1~3Q26EPS 4.07元；4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略高，毛利率將維持在
40%上下。 
發展策略將持續朝向⾼附加價值市場，2027年營收成⻑將⾼於20%：未來的
發展策略將持續朝向高附加價值市場邁進，包括發展精密力量控制技術（涵蓋
先進封裝Hybrid Bonding、高速固晶機等應用），大銀微將專注於系統整合
銷售，不單獨販售E1控制器，以確保軟硬體深度綁定，帶動營收與獲利持續同
步雙位數成⻑，2027年營收成⻑高於20%。 
 投資風險 
同業殺價競爭、地緣政治、匯率波動。

127%及-12%，全球半導體供應鏈重組與在地化採購需求強勁，3Q26營收12.2億元季增19%(年增77%)，高階定位平台
出貨佔比提升及規模經濟發揮，毛利率42.4%季增5.3pp(年增8.6pp)，規模經濟及費用控制得宜下，營業利益率24.3%
季增6.4pp(年增13.9pp)；稅前淨利3.3億元季增113%(年增255%)，EPS 2.15元，1~3Q26EPS 4.07元；毛利率提升主
要是產品結構及附加價值提升，奈微米定位平台由於高度客製化、整合空氣軸承、花崗岩平台、多軸線性馬達與高階雷
射補償演算法，且直接對接半導體⼀線客戶，毛利率達40~50%；精密運動及控制元件過去單賣馬達線圈與磁軌毛利率
未及30%，但大銀微力推「馬達＋自製E1系列驅動器＋控制器」整套搭配銷售，加上高單價晶圓移載用直驅力矩馬達出
貨放量，使元件部門毛利率亦同步墊高至30~35%。 
4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略⾼，毛利率將維持在40%上下： 4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略高，毛利率將維持在
40%上下，目前高階平台訂單能見度達5~6個月，交期已縮短至3個月；元件類能見度約3個月，交期約2~2.5個月，大
銀微具備85%的關鍵零組件自製率（包含線性馬達、驅動器、控制器），能快速打樣並提供高彈性交期。 
發展策略將持續朝向⾼附加價值市場，2027年營收成⻑將⾼於20%：未來的發展策略將持續朝向高附加價值市場邁進，
包括發展精密力量控制技術（涵蓋先進封裝Hybrid Bonding、高速固晶機等應用），大銀微將專注於系統整合銷售，不
單獨販售E1控制器，以確保軟硬體深度綁定，帶動營收與獲利持續同步雙位數成⻑，2027年營收成⻑高於20%；隨著AI

晶片尺寸逼近光罩極限，晶圓代工龍頭與OSAT廠加速推進FOPLP與矩陣式大面積封裝，大面積玻璃或有機基板在高速
取放、曝光與AOI檢測時，傳統滾珠螺桿會產生背隙與熱變形，必須全面改採「大跨距龍門雙驅無鐵芯線性馬達平台」
與「大型花崗岩空氣軸承系統」，大銀微在此領域具備完整大尺寸平台調校經驗，單機採用金額較傳統封裝設備提升2~3
倍；環繞式閘極與晶背供電大幅增加製程道數與光學/電子束檢測站點，歐美設備客戶為提升毛利率，持續將次系統委由
大銀微ODM，訂單能見度已直達2Q27；全球AI伺服器主板與交換器板廠在泰國大舉擴產，帶動高階雷射鑽孔機、機械
鑽孔機與成型機對大銀微線性馬達與高階驅動器的強勁拉貨；配合上銀(2049)集團佈局AI人型機器人，大銀微的高轉矩
密度無框力矩馬達與微型驅動器，具備切入機器人旋轉關節與靈巧⼿電控系統之⻑期潛力。 
CPO 相關營收訂單能見度已達 2H27：AI 資料中心算力互連由銅線轉向光學互連（800G/1.6T 光模組與 CPO），光
纖陣列（FAU）與矽光晶片波導進行主動對位（Active Alignment）時，要求定位精度必須達到奈米級且兼具極高反
應速度與抗震性，大銀微系統能解決高倍率顯微鏡檢測時的微小震動干擾，有效提升客戶設備產出率，主要應用於光
學引擎測試與系統級檢測，優勢在於可提供高精密、輕薄且體積緊湊的六軸定位平台，E1 控制器能將伺服穩定度提升
至正負 1 奈米，由於大銀的線性馬達是完全自行研發，因此可做到比業界體積更小，且可做到機電整合；大銀微開發
之多自由度奈米定位平台已切入台系與美系矽光子檢測及封裝設備鏈，2Q26 起開始交貨國內矽光子檢測設備大廠，
2H26 進⼀步放量，訂單能見度已達 2H27。 
2027 年資本支出為 1.2~1.5 億元，將新增 20~30%產能：面對全球半導體供應鏈區域化（美、日、歐晶圓廠擴建）
以及 AI 伺服器帶動高階 PCB/載板廠大舉移往泰國與東南亞設廠，大銀微採取「核心奈米平台留台灣台中擴產、標準
控制元件與次系統模組強化亞洲及歐美前線組裝」的雙軌策略，2H26 台中廠區針對高階微奈米定位系統與精密運動元
件已啟動擴產計畫，擴產產能規劃於 4Q26~1Q27 陸續完成，屆時將增加 20~30%的產能，進⼀步⽀撐 4Q26~1Q27
的出貨順暢度；第⼀階段產能擴建已完成，第二階段擴產預計於 2027 年 2 月底至 3 月中旬完成，並已開始規劃第三
階段，2027 年資本⽀出為 1.2~1.5 億元。

| 大銀微的定位與優勢 
大銀微掌握直驅馬達、線性馬達與驅動控制技術：大銀微系統成立於1997年，為上銀集團旗下的機電與超精密定位核心
企業，在集團分工體系中，⺟公司上銀（2049）負責提供高剛性機械結構與精密線性傳動部件，而大銀微則負責提供「大
腦（控制器）」、「神經（驅動器）」與「直接驅動心臟（線性馬達、力矩馬達、音圈馬達）」，大銀微與上銀為機電
整合雙引擎，大銀微掌握直驅馬達、線性馬達與驅動控制技術，而上銀專精於奈米與微米級的機械傳動（螺桿、線軌、
減速機）；大銀微的優勢包括馬達與驅動器與控制器「三合⼀」全自製能力(可針對奈米級平台進行底層演算法調校)、

### Page 4

4 
集團垂直整合綜效(結合上銀高精度螺桿/滑軌與自⾝線性馬達，提供⼀站式機電整合⽅案)、已通過全球前三大半導體設
備商及晶圓代工龍頭嚴苛認證。 
產業現狀：日本政府禁止高階工具機出口中國大陸，而中國大陸也管制高精度工具機出口，在全球貿易壁壘高築下，包
括北美、東南亞訂單大舉湧向台灣工具機業者；惟台灣工具機整機廠高階市場現面臨日本工具機廠挾日圓貶值優勢的威
脅，中低階或泛用型標準機又遭逢大陸業者低價搶市的威脅。 
產品應用範圍涵蓋半導體設備、面板及光電與自動化及PCB與載板設備等：大銀微的產品應用範圍涵蓋半導體設備(佔比
逾40%)、面板及光電與自動化（佔比約30~35%）、PCB與載板設備（佔比約20%）、精密量測及自動化、生技醫療等
產業，重量級客戶有應材（AMAT）、艾斯摩爾（ASML）、科磊（KLAC）及東京威力科創(8035.T)等全球前四大半導
體設備製造廠。

| 機器人產業簡介 
服務型機器人佔比較⾼，人形機器人正在起步：依據Precedence Research預估，服務型機器人市場2026年為725億美
元，2035年將可增加至2,338億美元，年均複合成⻑率為14%；工業型機器人市場2026年為307億美元，2035年將可增
加至933億美元，年均複合成⻑率為13%；人形機器人市場2026年為22億美元，2035年將可增加至88億美元，年均複
合成⻑率為35%，人形機器人雖然市場成⻑速度較快，但由於正在起步，市場規模及滲透率仍較低。 
機器人市場

資料來源 : Precedence Research、 玉山投顧整理、繪製 
人形機器人的結構與零組件

資料來源 : IEK、 玉山投顧整理、繪製

### Page 5

5 
AI模型讓機器人可持續學習優化：機器人智能可分為三個決策層、執行層及感知層三個主要層級，決策層負責環境理解、
任務規劃與行為決策；執行層由機器人本體與運動控制系統組成，包括伺服馬達、減速機、關節模組與控制器，負責把 
AI 的決策轉換成具體動作；感知層負責蒐集環境資訊，常見設備有RGB或RGB-D攝影機、雷射測距儀、力矩與觸覺感
測器，以及慣性量測單元，透過多模態感測資訊融合，系統才能對環境與物件有更精準的理解；AI模型讓機器人可持續
學習優化，隨著人形機器人與雙臂操作機器人快速發展，機構設計也愈來愈接近人類，如多自由度關節、靈巧⼿等設計，
讓機器人能在既有的人類工作環境中執行更複雜的任務。 
機器人智能系統主要層級包括決策層、執行層與感知層

資料來源 : 台達電 
控制器相當於機器人的大腦，AI時代更形重要：控制器的主要功能包括運動控制(計算多軸反向運動學，實現精準定位與
軌跡追蹤)、信號傳遞(將邏輯決策轉為電氣信號傳給驅動器與馬達)、安全防護(緊急停止與安全監控保護人員與設備)與
系統整合(連接視覺、力覺感測器以及工廠聯網通訊) ，AI時代將更形重要。 
AI時代控制器更形重要

資料來源 : 新代法說會簡報 
人機協作為目前工廠新典型，未來將走向智慧化：人機協作是指人類與人工智慧、機器人或自動化系統共同工作、互補
優勢以達成任務的模式，它透過機器處理大量數據與重複性工作，結合人類的創造力、同理心與終端決策，實現效率與
價值的最大化；核心優勢與分工在人機協作的模式下，人類與機器各司其職，發揮最強效能，機器擅⻑資料快速運算、
例行程序自動化、預測分析與24小時監控，而人類擅⻑戰略⽅向決策、複雜情境判斷、創意發想以及人際同理心溝通；
人機協作的共同效益為大幅降低人力閒置、提升組織生產力，並協助員工從低技術工作轉型為高附加價值工作，人機協
作為目前工廠新典型，但未來將走向智慧化，智慧化機器人結合了AI、先進感測器與精密控制系統，能自主感知周遭環
境、進行即時資料分析、做出決策，並執行原本需要人類智慧與物理動作才能完成的複雜任務，將AI實體化，其核心技

### Page 6

6 
術與特點在於將大腦演算法與實體機械結合，讓機器人具備空間理解與動態適應能力，並具有高精度感測與視覺，可透
過3D深度相機、光達與扭力感測器，做到精準避障與靈敏控制，由於具有模組化關節與驅動，透過高度整合馬達、驅動
器與通訊介面，提升扭力密度與即時反應速度；智慧化機器人主要應用領域智慧製造包括彈性產線、自動化組裝、設備
巡檢與物料搬運；醫療與照護⽅面如⽀援⼿術輔助、醫療物流與高齡化社會的照護陪伴；也能應用於特殊與極端環境，
如進入災害現場、地下空間或危險區域進行偵察與救援。 
協作型機器人市場2034年預估達713億美元

資料來源 : Precedence Research、 玉山投顧整理、繪製 
協作型機器人市場至2034年的年均複合成⻑率為35%：協作型機器人（Cobot）是⼀種專為與人類在共同工作空間內安
全協作而設計的機器人，與傳統工業機器人不同，其設計核心不是「最大化速度與力量」，而是「讓機器能夠安全地在
人的旁邊工作」，協作型機器人的概念最早在 1990 年代由學術界提出，2008年丹麥Universal Robots推出第⼀款商
業化Cobot協作型機器人後，這個品類才真正進入工業應用視野。此後十餘年間，隨著感測技術、控制演算法與製造成
本的成熟，協作型機器人快速普及，從歐美大廠擴展到亞洲製造業的中小型工廠。依據Precedence Research預估，協
作型機器人市場2025年為56億美元，2034年將可增加至713億美元，年均複合成⻑率為35%。

## Document 237: [005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up - 기업.pdf

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500 원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감한화에어로스페이스(012450) 방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실_16454052.pdf

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 11. 2  
방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang@lssec.co.kr

## Document 276: AI交易退潮、外资创纪录涌入！印度股市一度收复年内失地，全球资金开始重新押注印度

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易退潮、外资创纪录涌入！印度股市一度收复年内失地，全球资金开始重新押注印度.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

随着 AI 交易退潮，印度因 AI 敞⼝较低、以内需驱动⽽成为全球资⾦轮动的受益者，油价回
落也缓解了市场对其经常账⼾⾚字的担忧。 11 ⽉外资净流⼊已超 120 亿美元，有望创单⽉纪
录， MSCI 印度指数上涨逾 11% 。不过，外资全年仍为净卖出，反弹能否持续，最终取决于
企业盈利能否兑现增⻓预期。
摘要：
印度股市正迎来⼀场意外的反转。此前，印度因缺乏 AI 投资敞⼝⽽被全球资⾦冷落，如今却因同
样的原因重新受到⻘睐。随着拥挤的 AI 交易出现松动，全球投资者开始将⽬光转向印度这个以内
需为主要驱动⼒、科技股权重相对较低的新兴市场。
周⼀早盘，MSCI 印度指数⼀度上涨 0.6% ，在本⽉累计上涨 11% 的推动下，短暂收复 2026 年以
来的全部跌幅，随后回吐⽇内涨幅。相⽐之下， MSCI 新兴市场指数 11 ⽉以来已下跌约 10% ，此
前积累的年内涨幅也⾯临被抹去的⻛险。印度股市与新兴市场整体⾛势的分化，凸显出全球资
⾦配置⽅向正在发⽣变化。
资⾦流向也印证了这⼀转变。 11 ⽉以来，外资累计净流⼊印度股市超过 120 亿美元，有望创下单

⽉历史纪录。⾼盛、汇丰、花旗等机构本⽉相继上调印度股市评级。印度股市跻⾝全球本⽉表
现最佳市场之列，卢⽐也成为亚洲表现最好的货币之⼀。
AI 交易退潮，印度低敞⼝反成优势
过去数⽉，印度市场对 AI 投资热潮的参与度较低，⼀度成为其跑输全球股市的重要原因。由于缺
乏⼤型科技股和 AI 产业链企业，印度未能充分分享全球 AI ⾏情带来的上涨红利，资⾦持续流向科
技股权重更⾼的市场。
但随着 AI 交易⽇益拥挤，相关资产遭遇集中抛售，资⾦配置逻辑开始逆转。印度市场较低的 AI 敞
⼝，反⽽使其成为全球投资者分散⻛险、寻找新配置⽅向的选择。
Equinomics Research 创始⼈兼研究主管 G. Chokkalingam 表⽰，印度市场在此前⼀个⽉的悲观
情绪之后，已经具备反弹条件。油价下跌、外资⼤举回流，以及 AI 交易松动后全球基⾦对⼤型
内需市场和多元化投资机会的需求，共同推动了印度股市回暖。
从市场表现来看，这种资⾦轮动已经有所体现。本⽉ MSCI 印度指数上涨逾 11% ，⽽ MSCI 新兴

市场指数同期⼤幅下跌，两者⾛势明显背离。
油价回落叠加盈利改善，基本⾯提供⽀撑
除了全球资⾦轮动，印度市场⾃⾝的基本⾯也在改善。作为原油进⼝⼤国，印度对国际油价波
动较为敏感。近期油价下跌，缓解了市场对印度经常账⼾⾚字扩⼤的担忧，也为卢⽐和股市提
供了⽀撑。
与此同时，企业盈利表现也显⽰出韧性。据 Jefferies Financial Group 数据，MSCI 印度指数成分
股中，约 165 家公司在截⾄明年 3 ⽉的本财年预计实现 16% 的盈利增⻓，⾼于此前两个财年分别
12% 和 10% 的增速。
Emkay Global Financial Services 副研究主管 Avinash Singh 表⽰，企业盈利的稳健兑现正在改
善印度股市的⻛险回报⽐。

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
这意味着，印度股市此轮反弹并⾮完全依赖全球资⾦从 AI 交易撤出，油价回落和盈利增⻓预期改
善，也为市场提供了基本⾯⽀撑。
外资加速回流，印度股市反弹能否持续？
资⾦回流与基本⾯改善，正推动国际机构重新看好印度市场。多家机构本⽉相继上调印度股市
评级，美国银⾏则指出，主动型全球基⾦对印度的配置仍接近历史低位，意味着后续外资仍有
进⼀步增配的空间。
不过，短期资⾦流⼊能否转化为持续的市场上涨，仍存在不确定性。尽管 11 ⽉外资⼤举买⼊印
度股票，但从 2026 年全年看，境外投资者仍是净卖出⽅。NSE Nifty 50 指数周⼀下跌近 1% ，年
初⾄今仍下跌超 2% 。
更重要的是，印度股市⽬前受益于 AI 交易退潮、油价回落等外部因素，这些因素能否持续尚不确
定。随着全球资⾦轮动效应逐渐减弱，企业盈利能否继续兑现增⻓预期，将成为决定印度股市
反弹能否延续的关键。

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years.1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

growth, and solid earnings support
a constructive 2027 outlook, though
higher energy prices and emerging

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.
MSCI Emerging Markets c is a stock market index that tracks the performance of large-cap and mid-cap companies across 24 developing and fast-growing nations.
MSCI All-Country World Index ex USA is a market-capitalization-weighted stock market index that tracks large- and mid-cap equity performance across developed and emerging markets

worldwide, excluding the United States.
MSCI Europe, Australasia, and the Far East (EAFE) Index is a stock market index that measures the performance of large- and mid-cap companies across 21 developed.
Dow Jones Industrial Average Index is a stock market index that tracks 30 major public companies listed on the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) and Nasdaq.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is an alternative version of the standard S&P 500 that gives every constituent company an identical weight of 0.2% during each quarterly rebalance, rather

than weighting companies by their market capitalization.
Russell 2000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of approximately 2,000 small-cap (small-capitalization) public companies in the United States.
S&P 500 sub-sectors and industry groups Global Industry Classification Standard (GICS®)/S&P 500 Total Return Index, including Information Technology Total Return (TR) USD;
Consumer Discretionary TR USD; Industrials TR USD; Real Estate TR USD; Communication Services TR USD; Materials  TR USD; Financials TR USD; Consumer Staples TR USD; Utilities TR USD;

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《AI發債引發債務可持續性擔憂》
近期公債殖利率攀高引發市場對AI融資需求以及
企業償債能力的擔憂，AI產業鏈信用利差與CDS
擴大，不過多數大型科技企業多為投資級，美國
投資級AI產業鏈信用利差水準仍未突破今年高點；
此外統計今年以來大量發債的美國投資級債發行
人(淨發行量100億美元以上)共有17家，其中17%
的發行人信評獲調升，33%被下調，50%不變，
反映債務增加尚未造成普遍的債信壓力。
